This is coverage accounting, **not a claim that each function has been behaviorally verified**. See the main audit for findings and test limitations.

| server/load/test-assigned-workflow.cjs:76 | (async()=>{  const dbName='assigned_workflow_test_'+randomUUID().replaceAll('-','');  await mongoose.connect('mongodb://127.0.0.1:27028/?replicaSet=workflow-test',{dbName});  await Promise.all([User.init(),Property.init(),Inquiry.init(),Deal.init()]);  const server=createApp().listen(0,'127.0.0.1');await new Promise(r=>server.once('listening',r));  const base='http://127.0.0.1:'+server.address().port;let checks=0;  const req=async(path,actor,body,status=200,method=body?'POST':'GET',attempt=0)=>{const response=await fetch(base+path,{method,headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json',...(actor?{Authorization:'Bearer '+generateTokens(actor).accessToken}:{})},body:body?JSON.stringify(body):undefined});const text=await response.text();if(method==='GET'&&status===200&&response.status===503&&attempt<3){await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,100));return req(path,actor,body,status,method,attempt+1);}assert.equal(response.status,status,method+' '+path+' '+text);checks++;try{return text?JSON.parse(text):null;}catch{return text;}};  try {   const [owner,buyer,buyer2,advisor,verifier,other,admin,unverified]=await User.create(['user','user','user','advisor','verifier','advisor','admin','user'].map((role,i)=>({role,name:'Test '+i,mobile:'900111110'+i,passwordHash:'fixture-password-hash',isVerifiedIdentity:i!==7})));   const input={title:'Single reviewer listing',type:'land',location:{state:'Bihar',district:'Patna',lat:25.6,lng:85.1},price:100000,totalArea:100,sellableArea:100,media:{photos:[],videos:[]}};   const report={decision:'approve',notes:'Ownership and site checked',signature:'Test Staff',checklist:{ownership:true,location:true,boundaries:true,area:true,disclosures:true,documentsReviewed:true}};   async function listing(requested){const p=await req('/properties',owner,{...input,...(requested?{requestedAdvisorId:String(requested._id)}:{})},201);const form=new FormData();form.append('uploadId',randomUUID());form.append('file',new Blob([Buffer.from([255,216,255,1,2,3])],{type:'image/jpeg'}),'fixture.jpg');const uploaded=await fetch(base+'/properties/'+p._id+'/photos',{method:'POST',headers:{Authorization:'Bearer '+generateTokens(owner).accessToken},body:form});assert.equal(uploaded.status,201);await req('/properties/'+p._id+'/submit',owner,{consent:true},202);await processNextSubmission();return Property.findById(p._id);}   const p=await listing(advisor);assert.equal(String(p.assignedAdvisorId),String(advisor._id));assert.equal(p.assignedVerifierId,null);   await req('/verification/'+p._id+'/report',other,report,403);   await req('/verification/'+p._id+'/report',advisor,report);   await req('/properties/'+p._id);   const v=await listing();assert.equal(String(v.assignedVerifierId),String(verifier._id));await req('/verification/'+v._id+'/report',verifier,report);await req('/properties/'+v._id);   await req('/deals/'+p._id+'/offer',null,{amount:90000},401);   await req('/deals/'+p._id+'/offer',unverified,{amount:90000},403);   const chat=await req('/inquiries',buyer,{propertyId:String(p._id),message:'Interested',offerAmount:90000,requestKey:'workflow_offer_retry_key'},201);   assert.equal(String(chat.staffId),String(advisor._id));assert.ok(chat.dealId);   const repeated=await req('/inquiries',buyer,{propertyId:String(p._id),message:'Interested',offerAmount:90000,requestKey:'workflow_offer_retry_key'},201);assert.equal(repeated._id,chat._id);   await req('/recommendations/preferences',buyer,{district:'Patna',type:'land',maxPrice:150000},204);   await req('/users/wishlist/'+p._id,unverified,{});   await req('/users/kyc',unverified);   const kycForm=new FormData();kycForm.append('files',new Blob(['%PDF-1.4 test identity fixture'],{type:'application/pdf'}),'fixture.pdf');   const upload=await fetch(base+'/users/kyc',{method:'POST',headers:{Authorization:'Bearer '+generateTokens(unverified).accessToken},body:kycForm});assert.equal(upload.status,202);checks++;   const kyc=await req('/users/kyc',unverified);assert.equal(kyc.kycStatus,'pending');   await req('/users/kyc/'+kyc.kycDocuments[0],buyer,undefined,404);   await req('/users/kyc/'+kyc.kycDocuments[0]+'/download',buyer,{},404);   const ticket=await req('/users/kyc/'+kyc.kycDocuments[0]+'/download',admin,{});   const downloadPath=new URL(ticket.url).pathname+new URL(ticket.url).search;   await req(downloadPath,null);   await req(downloadPath,null,undefined,403);    await req('/admin/users/'+unverified._id+'/identity',admin,{approved:true,notes:'Identity documents reviewed and approved'},200,'PUT');   await req('/inquiries/'+chat._id,owner,undefined,403);await req('/inquiries/'+chat._id,other,undefined,403);   await req('/inquiries/'+chat._id+'/message',advisor,{text:'Welcome'});   await req('/inquiries/'+chat._id,buyer);   const second=await req('/deals/'+p._id+'/offer',buyer2,{amount:95000},201);   await req('/deals/'+chat.dealId+'/status',owner,{status:'accepted'},403,'PATCH');   await req('/deals/'+chat.dealId+'/status',advisor,{status:'countered',counterAmount:98000},200,'PATCH');   await req('/deals/'+chat.dealId+'/status',buyer,{status:'accepted'},200,'PATCH');   await req('/deals/'+second._id+'/status',advisor,{status:'accepted'},409,'PATCH');   await req('/deals/'+chat.dealId+'/status',buyer,{status:'completed'},403,'PATCH');   await req('/deals/'+chat.dealId+'/status',advisor,{status:'completed'},200,'PATCH');   await req('/deals/'+chat.dealId+'/status',advisor,{status:'completed'},200,'PATCH');   assert.equal((await Property.findById(p._id)).status,'sold');assert.equal((await Deal.findById(second._id)).status,'cancelled');   await req('/deals/'+p._id+'/offer',buyer2,{amount:95000},409);   await req('/inquiries/'+chat._id+'/message',buyer,{text:'new offer'},409);   await req('/properties/'+p._id,undefined,undefined,404);   for(const mode of ['new','trending','personalized']){const items=await req('/recommendations?mode='+mode);assert.ok(!items.some(x=>x._id===String(p._id)));}   const vc=await req('/inquiries',buyer,{propertyId:String(v._id),message:'Verifier chat'},201);await req('/inquiries/'+vc._id+'/message',verifier,{text:'I handle this property'});   await req('/admin/properties/'+v._id+'/assign',admin,{staffId:String(other._id)});   await req('/inquiries/'+vc._id,verifier,undefined,403);await req('/inquiries/'+vc._id,other);   const race=await listing(advisor);await req('/verification/'+race._id+'/report',advisor,report);   const ra=await req('/deals/'+race._id+'/offer',buyer,{amount:80000},201),rb=await req('/deals/'+race._id+'/offer',buyer2,{amount:85000},201);   const results=await Promise.all([ra,rb].map(d=>fetch(base+'/deals/'+d._id+'/status',{method:'PATCH',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json',Authorization:'Bearer '+generateTokens(advisor).accessToken},body:JSON.stringify({status:'accepted'})}).then(r=>r.status)));   assert.deepEqual(results.sort(),[200,409]);checks+=2;   console.log('PASS: '+checks+' HTTP checks plus assignment, privacy, reservation, sale and recommendation assertions');  } finally {await new Promise(r=>server.close(r));await mongoose.connection.dropDatabase();await mongoose.disconnect();} })().catch callback | — |

  let server;   try {     await Promise.all([User.init(), PasswordReset.init()]);     const user = await User.create({ role: 'user', name: 'Reset Test', email: 'reset@example.test', emailVerifiedAt: new Date(), passwordHash: await passwords.hash('Original!Forest82', 10) });     const app = express(); app.use(express.json()); app.use('/auth', router);     server = app.listen(0, '127.0.0.1'); await new Promise(resolve => server.once('listening', resolve));     const post = async (path, body) => {       const r = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}/auth/${path}`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(body) });       return { status: r.status, data: await r.json() };     };     const request = () => post('forgot-password', { identifier: user.email });     let result = await request(); assert.equal(result.status, 200);     const first = result.data.challengeId;     assert.ok(!result.data.destination.includes('&'));     assert.equal((await request()).status, 200);     const reset = (challengeId, code = '123456') => post('reset-password', { challengeId, code, newPassword: 'New!Forest82Secure' });     assert.equal((await reset(first, '000000')).status, 400);     assert.equal((await reset(first, '1234567')).status, 400);     assert.ok(await passwords.compare('Original!Forest82', (await User.findById(user._id)).passwordHash));     await PasswordReset.updateOne({ challengeId: first }, { $set: { expiresAt: new Date(0), resendAt: new Date(0) } });     assert.equal((await reset(first)).status, 400);     const resends = await Promise.all([post('forgot-password/resend', { challengeId: first }), post('forgot-password/resend', { challengeId: first })]);     assert.ok(resends.every(r => r.status === 200));     const second = (await PasswordReset.findById(String(user._id))).challengeId;     assert.equal(resends.filter(r => r.data.challengeId === second).length, 1);     assert.notEqual(first, second);     assert.equal((await reset(first)).status, 400);     // A Verify challenge must never accept the local digest, even if it matches.     await PasswordReset.updateOne({ challengeId: second }, { $set: { provider: 'twilio-verify' } });     assert.equal((await reset(second)).status, 400);     await PasswordReset.updateOne({ challengeId: second }, { $set: { provider: 'local' } });     const results = await Promise.all([reset(second), reset(second)]);     assert.deepEqual(results.map(r => r.status).sort(), [200, 400]);     assert.equal((await reset(second)).status, 400);     assert.ok(await passwords.compare('New!Forest82Secure', (await User.findById(user._id)).passwordHash));     assert.equal((await post('login', { email: user.email, password: 'Original!Forest82' })).status, 401);     assert.equal((await post('login', { email: user.email, password: 'New!Forest82Secure' })).status, 200);     delete process.env.OTP_STUB_CODE;     process.env.TWILIO_ACCOUNT_SID = 'AC' + '1'.repeat(32);     process.env.TWILIO_AUTH_TOKEN = 'test-token';     process.env.TWILIO_VERIFY_SERVICE_SID = 'VA' + '2'.repeat(32);     process.env.TWILIO_PHONE_NUMBER = '';     const realFetch = global.fetch;     let verificationChecks = 0;     global.fetch = async (url, options) => {       if (!String(url).startsWith('https://verify.twilio.com/')) return realFetch(url, options);       if (String(url).endsWith('/VerificationCheck')) {         verificationChecks++;         return Response.json({ status: new URLSearchParams(options.body).get('Code') === '654321' ? 'approved' : 'pending' });       }       return Response.json({ sid: 'VE' + '3'.repeat(32) });     };     try {       const mobileUser = await User.create({ role: 'user', name: 'SMS Reset', mobile: '9000000002', mobileVerifiedAt: new Date(), passwordHash: await passwords.hash('Original!Forest82', 10) });       const sms = await post('forgot-password', { identifier: mobileUser.mobile });       assert.equal(sms.status, 200);       assert.equal((await PasswordReset.findOne({ challengeId: sms.data.challengeId })).provider, 'twilio-verify');       assert.equal((await reset(sms.data.challengeId, '000000')).status, 400);       assert.equal((await reset(sms.data.challengeId, '654321')).status, 200);       assert.equal(verificationChecks, 2);     } finally { global.fetch = realFetch; }     console.log('PASS: email-only reset, invalid/expired OTP, resend throttling/concurrency, provider binding, single use, and password replacement');   } finally {     if (server) await new Promise(resolve => server.close(resolve));     await mongoose.connection.dropDatabase(); await mongoose.disconnect();   } })().catch callback | — |

    assert.deepEqual(checks.map(r => r.status).sort(), [201, 400]);     assert.ok(checks.find(r => r.status === 201).data.accessToken);     assert.equal(await User.countDocuments(), 1);     assert.ok((await User.findOne()).mobileVerifiedAt);     assert.ok((await User.findOne()).refreshTokenExpiresAt > new Date());     assert.equal((await verify(third)).status, 400);     assert.equal((await post('/auth/login', details)).status, 200);     const savedToken = process.env.TWILIO_AUTH_TOKEN;     assert.equal((await post('/auth/register', { ...details, mobile: '9000000002' })).status, 503);     assert.equal(await User.countDocuments(), 1);     failSend = false;     process.env.TWILIO_SMS_TEST_MODE = 'true'; "googleHybrid" : "googleStreet");          googleMapRef.current = map;         setMapEngine("google");          map.addListener("tilesloaded", () => {           const errorAnchors = document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(             'a[href*="error-messages"], a[href*="developers.google.com/maps"], .dismissButton'           );             }             if (curr && curr !== document.body) {               curr.remove();             }           });         });          // Map Click to Add New Boundary Point         map.addListener("click", (e: any) => {           if (isClosedRef.current && pointsRef.current.length >= 3) return;            const newPt: LatLngPoint = { lat: e.latLng.lat(), lng: e.latLng.lng() };           const previous = pointsRef.current;            // Clicking near the first vertex closes an existing polygon           if (previous.length >= 3 && getEdgeDistance(previous[0], newPt).feet < 30) {             setIsDrawingClosed(true);             isClosedRef.current = true;             if (googleRubberBandRef.current) {               googleRubberBandRef.current.setMap(null);               googleRubberBandRef.current = null;             }             setCursorDistanceFt(null);             return;           }            const updated = [...previous, newPt];           setHistory((h) => [...h, previous]);           setRedoHistory([]);           pointsRef.current = updated;           setPoints(updated);         });          // Live Rubber-Band Guide Line to Mouse Cursor         map.addListener("mousemove", (e: any) => {           const curPts = pointsRef.current;           if (curPts.length > 0 && !isClosedRef.current) {             const lastPt = curPts[curPts.length - 1];             const mousePt = { lat: e.latLng.lat(), lng: e.latLng.lng() };              if (!googleRubberBandRef.current) {               googleRubberBandRef.current = new maps.Polyline({                 path: [lastPt, mousePt],                 strokeColor: "#10B981",                 strokeWeight: 2,                 strokeOpacity: 0.85,                 map,                 clickable: false,               });             } else {               googleRubberBandRef.current.setPath([lastPt, mousePt]);             }              const dist = getEdgeDistance(lastPt, mousePt);             setCursorDistanceFt(dist.feet);
